We show that such history can hijack a policy the model already possesses: on Qwen3-1.7B, pollution flips 32.1% of decisions that are correct under the original trajectory and frequently induces reuse of corrupted entities or interface conventions. We introduce bench, a paired benchmark with synchronized Original, Polluted, and Oracle State views that preserve the system policy, current tools, latest request, and gold next action. Eleven gold-preserving interventions isolate failures in decision state, entity binding, and interface execution across complete calls and non-call decisions. We further propose ours, which transfers an Oracle-conditioned teacher policy to a student observing only polluted history through soft supervision on student-generated prefixes. On Qwen3-1.7B, ours achieves 87.0% Balanced Tool-Use Accuracy, outperforming Gold-SFT (66.3%), Oracle sequence distillation (82.3%), and off-policy token distillation (85.0%). The method scales consistently: an 8B teacher raises the same compact 1.7B student to 91.9%, while an 8B student reaches 93.0%. The resulting policies further transfer to clean histories, unseen functions, independently regenerated evaluation contexts, external tool-use benchmarks, and noisy multi-hop question answering. These results establish history reliability as a distinct tool-use bottleneck and demonstrate reliable-state policy transfer as an effective and scalable solution.

</details>


### [248] [From Siloed Algorithms to Compliance-First Agentic Platforms: A Multi-Layered Architecture for Hospital AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06112)
*Manideep Dhar,Ritwik Singh,Sharat Chandra Kumar Manikonda*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种面向医院的合规优先型Agentic AI架构，旨在解决医疗AI部署孤立、扩展失败率高的问题。该架构通过智能体编排、集中化合规策略和隐私保护数据层，实现了从试点到企业级平台的规模化转型，显著提升了效率并确保了全球合规性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管医院广泛采用AI，但大多数部署仍局限于部门孤岛，导致重复工作、隐藏风险且未能实现企业价值。数据显示70-80%的医疗AI试点因治理缺失、数据碎片化和缺乏集成蓝图而无法扩展，亟需一种系统化、合规且可互操作的解决方案。

Method: 研究提出了一种包含三个可互操作层的医院特定Agentic AI架构：(1) 智能体编排层，用于跨临床、运营和财务领域的多智能体工作流；(2) 合规与政策层，将HIPAA、GDPR、欧盟AI法案等法规转化为代码进行集中化管理；(3) 隐私保护数据层，集成联邦学习、差分隐私和安全飞地技术以适配医院信息系统(HIMS)。研究使用合成但结构真实的医院数据集及可部署的原型系统进行验证。

Result: 原型系统成功实现了分诊风险预测、工作流优化和合规日志记录的端到端编排。模拟结果显示，任务周转时间和手动文档工作量大幅减少，同时保持了受政策保护的数据访问权限，证明了架构在提升效率和安全性方面的有效性。

Conclusion: 该架构为医院领导者提供了一个务实的蓝图，使其能够从临时的工具转向受治理、全球合规且注重投资回报率的AI平台。该方案支持定制化的本地、混合及云原生部署，有助于解决当前医疗AI规模化扩展的瓶颈。

Abstract: Hospitals are rapidly adopting artificial intelligence for triage, imaging, scheduling etc., yet most deployments remain isolated point solutions locked inside departmental silos, resulting in duplicated effort, hidden risks, and unrealized enterprise value. Despite explosive growth of AI in healthcare market and accelerating investment, an estimated 70-80% of healthcare AI pilots fail to scale, largely due to governance gaps, fragmented data, and missing integration blueprints. This research proposes a hospital-specific, compliance-first, Agentic AI architecture with multiple interoperable layers, extending existing hospital AI platform models with: (i) an Agent Orchestration Layer for multi-agent workflows across clinical, operational, and financial domains, (ii) a Compliance and Policy Layer that centralizes policy-as-code for HIPAA, GDPR, the EU AI Act, DISHA Act, India's DPDP Act, and ISO/IEC security and safety standards, and (iii) a Privacy-Preserving Data Fabric that plugs federated learning, differential privacy, and secure enclaves into real-world Hospital Information Management System (HIMS) flows. Using a synthetic but structurally realistic hospital dataset and an open, ready-to-deploy prototype implementation, this study demonstrates the end-to-end orchestration of triage risk prediction, workflow optimization, and compliance logging, achieving substantial simulated reductions in task turnaround times and manual documentation effort while maintaining policy-guarded data access. The resulting architecture offers hospital leaders a pragmatic blueprint to move from ad hoc tools to a governed, globally compliant, ROI-focused AI platform that can be tailored to on-premise, hybrid and cloud-native deployments.

</details>


### [249] [Signal or Spurious Cue? A Randomized Audit of Survey-Country Metadata in LLM Social Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06085)
*Yifan Lyu,Xinran Li,Jiaqi Qiao,Xiujuan Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究通过审计实验发现，虽然经过验证的真实国家元数据能显著降低大语言模型（LLM）预测的误差，但仅仅披露随机标签的来源并不能有效消除模型对该虚假线索的依赖，表明透明度不足以完全纠正模型对误导性信息的利用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管国家元数据在信息丰富时能提升LLM对个人回答的预测能力，但当该线索被随机分配时，可能会误导模型的预测方向。研究旨在测试披露随机标签的均匀、独立来源是否能减少模型对其的采纳，并验证真实国家信息对预测准确性的实际影响。

Method: 采用记录内审计方法，使用5个固定API模型、6个国家及7个开发选定的目标进行测试。主要面板包含72条记录，对比了不透明标签、披露的随机标签和已验证的国家信息。通过独立的人口锚点和人类回答记录来测量预测的方向和后果，并使用Brier损失评估预测精度。

Result: 1. 不透明标签和披露的随机标签均导致0.214的国家方向偏移，且披露并未显著减弱这种偏移（配对衰减为0.0003，置信区间包含零）。
2. 已验证的国家信息将Brier损失降低了0.040（95% CI [0.024, 0.056]），显示出显著的有用性。
3. 在非重叠的一致性面板中，已验证元数据依然有用，但披露仍未可靠地削弱随机标签的影响。
4. 发布了包含14,400个配对项目级概率分布的数据集PROV-FORECAST。

Conclusion: 已验证的元数据能有效提高LLM预测的准确性，但仅靠披露随机标签的来源无法可靠地阻止模型利用这些误导性线索。这表明在减少LLM对虚假或无关元数据的依赖方面，透明度措施存在局限性。

Abstract: Survey-country metadata can improve an LLM's forecast of an individual response when informative, yet the same cue may redirect the forecast when assigned at random. A within-record audit tests whether disclosing a random label's uniform, record-independent origin reduces its country-directed uptake, and whether verified survey country lowers held-out Brier loss. Independent population anchors and recorded human answers measure direction and consequence across five fixed API models, six countries, and seven development-selected targets. In the primary post-review 72-record panel, opaque and disclosed-random labels each produced country-direction shifts of 0.214. Paired attenuation was 0.0003 (95% CI [-0.0157, 0.0166]). Verified country reduced Brier loss by 0.040 (95% CI [0.024, 0.056]), while random-label regret included zero. A non-overlapping mixed-coverage consistency panel retained positive disclosed-random movement and verified utility, while attenuation remained uncertain. On the selected targets, verified metadata was useful in both panels, but disclosure did not reliably attenuate random-label uptake. PROV-FORECAST contains 14,400 paired item-level probability distributions from the corrected panel.

</details>


### [250] [Poli-Bias: Understanding and Measuring Large Language Model Biases in International Political Conflicts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06123)
*Massi-Nissa Abboud,Aladin Djuhera,Elena Cabrio,Holger Boche*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Poli-Bias框架，通过对比法替换国家身份的配对提示，评估13种大语言模型在处理法律等效冲突场景时是否存在基于国家的政治偏见，并将偏差分解为五个维度进行细粒度审计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于政治偏见可能通过细微的措辞、论证和法律推理差异体现，难以用单一指标捕捉，因此需要一种更精细的方法来测量大型语言模型（LLMs）中的政治偏见。

Method: 引入Poli-Bias反事实框架，通过系统性地交换配对提示中的国家身份，涵盖不同的地缘政治关系、法律违规行为和推理任务，将响应差异分解为五个可解释的维度。

Result: 在13种不同模型系列和大小的当代LLM中发现，国家身份和用户隶属关系会系统地影响对国际法下等效行为的描述、评估和辩护方式。

Conclusion: Poli-Bias确立了一种用于审计LLMs政治公正性和阿谀奉承行为的细粒度框架。

Abstract: Measuring political bias in large language models (LLMs) remains challenging as it can manifest through subtle differences in framing, argumentation, and legal reasoning that are difficult to capture with a single metric. In this work, we introduce Poli-Bias, a counterfactual framework for measuring whether LLMs treat legally equivalent conflict scenarios differently depending on the countries involved. Poli-Bias compares responses to paired prompts in which country identities are systematically swapped across diverse geopolitical relationships, legal violations, and reasoning tasks. Rather than reducing bias to a single judgment, our framework decomposes response disparities into five interpretable dimensions, revealing how and where unequal treatment manifests. Across 13 contemporary LLMs spanning diverse model families and sizes, we find that country identities and user affiliations can systematically affect how equivalent actions are described, evaluated, and defended under international law. Our results thus establish Poli-Bias as a fine-grained framework for auditing political even-handedness and sycophancy in LLMs.

</details>


### [251] [Evaluating Investment Logic in Large Language Models: A Real-World Benchmark Towards Personalzied Financial Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06108)
*Yuanhong Jiang,Jingjie Zou,Zhenghong Lin,Xusheng Yu,Qiqi Huang,Shuai Jia,Shijie Dai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出InvestLogicBench基准，通过P→E→R→D→O轨迹评估金融LLM的逻辑合理性与事件 grounding 能力，发现现有模型虽逻辑看似合理但缺乏真实市场依据，指出仅看盈亏或静态问答的评估缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前金融大语言模型（LLMs）的评估方式存在局限：静态问答忽略投资者主体性，而仅看最终盈亏无法区分盈利是源于合理的、符合个人画像的投资逻辑还是运气，且未考虑投资能力的个性化特征。

Method: 构建包含151位真实投资者201,247条决策记录的InvestLogicBench基准。采用P→E→R→D→O（画像-事件-推理-决策-结果）轨迹结构，支持理解、基于画像生成及端到端回放，并在四个主流LLMs上进行测试。

Result: 在四个领先LLMs中，逻辑合理性评分接近4/5，但事件 grounding（即决策是否基于真实市场证据）评分极低（0.8--2.8/5）。回报与过程质量评估结果不一致，揭示了模型“表面合理但根基薄弱”的推理问题，这是仅依赖结果评估所掩盖的。

Conclusion: 建议将P→E→R→D→O作为数据系统接口标准，要求版本化画像、时间溯源、可检查检索、决策账本和可回放结果。该研究以金融为压力测试，旨在推动更广泛的个性化、后果敏感型智能体评估体系的发展。

Abstract: Investment competence is inherently personalized: the same market evidence can justify different actions for investors with different goals, horizons, portfolios, and risk boundaries. Yet financial LLMs are evaluated either by static question answering or by terminal profit and loss. The former omits agency; the latter cannot reveal whether a profitable action was grounded, profile-consistent, or merely lucky. We ask whether the community is using the wrong ruler for consequential agents.
  We introduce \textsc{InvestLogicBench}, a process-native benchmark containing 201,247 documented decisions from 151 real-world investors. Each episode instantiates a \textbf{P$\rightarrow$E$\rightarrow$R$\rightarrow$D$\rightarrow$O} trace: investor \textit{Profile}, observable market \textit{Events}, investment \textit{Reasoning}, executable \textit{Decision}, and delayed \textit{Outcome}. The release includes profile construction, point-in-time event binding, structured logic, horizons, outcomes, and post-mortems, and supports comprehension, profile-conditioned generation, and end-to-end replay. Across four leading LLMs, logical plausibility remains near 4/5 while event grounding is only 0.8--2.8/5; return and process quality also disagree. These results expose polished but weakly grounded reasoning that outcome-only evaluation hides. We further argue that P$\rightarrow$E$\rightarrow$R$\rightarrow$D$\rightarrow$O should be a data-system interface, requiring versioned profiles, temporal provenance, inspectable retrieval, decision ledgers, and replayable outcomes. Finance is our stress test for a broader class of personalized, consequential agents.

</details>


### [252] [Schema-Guided Hierarchical Information Extraction and Semantic Evaluation Using Generative AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06167)
*Modhurita Mitra,Jan-Willem Versteeg,Maarten D. Schermer,Shiva Nadi Najafabadi,Marie L. De Bruin,Lourens T. Bloem*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种基于模式（schema-based）的框架，利用生成式AI从零样本、单次调用的方式从非结构化文本中提取复杂嵌套信息，并通过引入路径语义匹配算法和Rubric分类标准进行自动化语义评估。在NICE文档测试中，使用Claude Opus 3模型实现了14个属性中12个超过90%的F1分数，提取效率比人类专家快30倍，并验证了跨模型、跨组织和跨语言的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决从非结构化文本中提取复杂、结构化信息（如层级嵌套、可变基数属性）的困难，以及缺乏针对此类提取结果的统一、系统化且一致的自动语义评估方法的问题。

Method: 1. 构建基于模式的信息模型以编码领域知识，指导信息的提取与评估；
2. 采用零样本（zero-shot）模式下的一次模型调用完成信息提取；
3. 开发基于路径的语义匹配算法，将提取结果中的嵌套属性与金标准对齐；
4. 利用生成式AI进行属性值的语义比较，并根据领域特定考量通过Rubric将结果分类为精确匹配、语义匹配、有用匹配或不匹配。

Result: 在使用健康技术评估组织NICE发布的文档进行测试时，成功提取了14个属性中的12个，F1分数大于90%。相比人类领域专家，该方法的提取时间缩短了约30倍。实验还证明了该框架在不同生成式AI模型间的通用性，以及在HTA组织和语言之间的可迁移性。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了一种高效、准确且可扩展的方法，用于从非结构化文本中提取复杂结构信息并进行自动化评估，具有显著的效率优势良好的泛化性能。

Abstract: We present a schema-based framework for extracting complex, structured information from unstructured text documents using generative AI, followed by automated semantic evaluation of the extracted information against a gold standard. The schema, serving as an information model encoding domain knowledge, provides a unified, systematic, and consistent framework for extraction of hierarchical, nested information, with attributes of variable cardinality, and subsequent evaluation of the results. Information extraction from a document is performed in a single call to the model, in zero-shot mode.
  In the evaluation step, we introduce a path-based semantic matching algorithm to align the nested, variable-cardinality attributes in the extracted results with those in the gold standard. We use generative AI for semantic comparison of the extracted and gold standard values of an attribute, and introduce a rubric to classify the result of the comparison, according to domain-specific considerations, as an exact, semantic, useful, or non-match.
  We were able to extract 12 out of 14 attributes with an F1 score of $>$90\% from documents published by the health technology assessment organisation NICE, using the generative AI model Claude Opus 3. The time needed to extract the attributes from a document was $\sim$30 times lower than the time taken by a human domain expert. We further demonstrate generalisability of this framework across different generative AI models and transferability across different HTA organisations and languages.

</details>


### [253] [HarnessOpt-Bench: Evaluating LLMs at Harness Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06301)
*Varun Ursekar,Apaar Shanker,Yash Maurya,Shehab Yasser,Vijay S. Kalmath,Veronica Chatrath,Yuan Xue*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出HarnessOpt-Bench基准，用于评估前沿大语言模型在复杂、随机且昂贵的评估环境下进行端到端Agent Harness优化的能力。实验表明，优化器模型本身的差异比其使用的编码Harness影响更大，该能力具有显著的提升空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM在智能体系统中的应用增加，系统的性能不仅取决于模型权重，还取决于“Harness”（提示、工具、控制流等）。目前缺乏衡量前沿LLM在此任务上表现的通用协议，亟需一种自动化Harness优化的评估标准。

Method: 构建HarnessOpt-Bench基准：优化器（LLM+编码Harness）接收种子Harness和反馈，在固定预算内迭代编辑Harness并提名最终候选者。通过可信执行环境（TEE）确保评估边界、资源计量及审计追踪。在4个下游任务上进行111次评分运行，对比共享编码Harness与原生Harness的表现。

Result: 优化器模型之间的性能差异远大于它们所通过的编码Harness之间的差异；原生Harness并未始终表现出优势；不同任务和种子机制下的增益变化显著。

Conclusion: 确立了Harness优化作为一种可测量且具有区分度的能力，当前存在巨大的改进空间，为未来AI系统自动化优化提供了重要基准。

Abstract: As LLMs are increasingly deployed within agentic systems, their capabilities depend not only on the model weights but also on the harness: the prompts, tools, control flow, memory, and orchestration code surrounding them. This makes automated harness optimization -- the iterative and evaluation-guided improvement of a harness by an AI system -- both an important route to improving AI systems and a demanding capability for AI systems themselves. Yet the community lacks a common protocol for measuring how well frontier LLMs perform at this task. We introduce HarnessOpt-Bench, a benchmark for end-to-end harness optimization under expensive and stochastic evaluation. An optimizer, an LLM paired with a coding harness, receives a target agent's seed harness, graded evaluation feedback, and a fixed target-evaluation budget. It edits the harness and nominates a final candidate, which is scored by its normalized gain over the seed on a held-out test partition that remains inaccessible throughout search. A trusted execution environment enforces the evaluation boundary, meters target-agent resource use, and preserves candidate versions for audit. We evaluate 5 frontier LLMs as optimizers both under a shared coding harness and under their native harnesses across 4 downstream tasks, over 111 scored runs. Experiment results show that optimizer models separate more than the coding harnesses they act through, native harnesses are not consistently superior, and gains vary substantially across tasks and seed regimes. These results establish harness optimization as a measurable and discriminative capability with large space for improvement.

</details>


### [254] [Mind the Gaps: Mixture-of-Minds for Human Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06115)
*Pranav Dahiya*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Anacreon，一种针对个体层面的受众模拟模型。通过在Gemma 4 12B基础上训练专用适配器，结合情感链增强和偏差平衡策略，实现了个体预测精度的显著提升（序数对齐达0.775），克服了传统大语言模型在个体层面缺乏异质性和存在社会偏见的缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决现有统计方法和大型语言模型模拟器在预测个体反应时的局限性：虽然能捕捉群体总体趋势，但会抹平个体差异（异质性），并携带社会偏见和提示脆弱性，导致个体预测失真。

Method: 1. 构建作者嵌入以区分个体，围绕种子人物对真实定性语料进行聚类；2. 为每个聚类训练专用适配器（思维混合体）；3. 从公共文本中提取人口统计学、心理特征和调查回应，并添加“情感链”进行数据增强；4. 通过打乱选项减少提示脆弱性，通过平衡训练分布减少正向偏差。

Result: 在大规模外部来源调查中，Anacreon达到了0.775的序数对齐分数，这是该领域公认的个体层面准确性指标，且残差偏差较小，优于现有基准。

Conclusion: Anacreon通过忠实模拟具有异质性的个体，为从模拟个体中推导聚合洞察迈出了重要一步，证明了在狭窄且定义明确的领域内实现高精度个体模拟的可行性。

Abstract: Predicting how a population will answer a new question is a long-standing goal. Statistical methods succeed at the level of the mass but falter at the level of the individual. Large language model simulators inherit this gap. They recover a population's central tendencies while flattening its heterogeneity, and they carry social biases and prompt brittleness that distort individual predictions. This paper introduces Anacreon, an audience simulation model that targets the individual level within a narrow, well-specified domain. Anacreon learns an authorship embedding that separates individuals, clusters a real qualitative corpus around seed people, and trains a dedicated adapter for each cluster, a mixture of minds, on a Gemma~4 12B base. It harvests demographics, psychological traits, and survey responses from public text, and augments each record with a chain-of-emotion. It reduces prompt brittleness by shuffling response options and reduces positive bias by balancing the training distribution. On a large, externally sourced survey, Anacreon reaches a state-of-the-art ordinal alignment of 0.775, the individual-level accuracy measure on which the field has converged, with a small residual bias. The work is a step toward drawing aggregate insight from faithfully simulated individuals.

</details>


### [255] [Contextual Information Policy Optimization for Search Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06128)
*Xingyu Guo,Wei Chen,Linlin Yang,Baochang Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出CIPO框架，通过强化学习将策略优化与外部证据使用对齐，解决大语言模型搜索代理中的确认偏误问题，提升多步推理中对外部信息的利用效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有搜索代理方法主要奖励最终答案正确性或中间进展，未直接评估检索后行动是否基于检索到的证据，导致“先验驱动推理”（prior-driven reasoning），即代理主要依赖内部知识形成结论并用检索来确认，产生确认偏误和证据利用低效。

Method: 提出上下文信息策略优化（Contextual Information Policy Optimization, CIPO），一种面向证据的强化学习框架。该方法为受检索信息影响的推理动作分配密集的回合级信用（dense, turn-level credit），并将此证据使用信号与全局结果奖励结合，以保留答案正确性。无需人工过程标注或额外的奖励模型。

Result: 在七个域内和域外基准测试上进行广泛实验，结果显示CIPO减少了先验驱动推理的发生率，并在大多数任务上取得了优异的性能。

Conclusion: CIPO通过显式对齐策略优化与外部证据使用，有效解决了搜索代理中的确认偏误问题，促进了由检索事实引导或修正后续推理的推理轨迹，提升了复杂信息任务下的可靠性。

Abstract: Search agents extend large language models beyond static parametric memory by enabling them to acquire and use ex ternal evidence during multi-step reasoning. For knowledge intensive tasks involving complex or evolving information, their reliability depends not only on retrieving relevant ev idence but also on using it to guide subsequent reasoning. However, existing methods primarily reward final-answer cor rectness or intermediate progress, without directly assessing whether post-retrieval actions are grounded in the retrieved evidence. This misalignment encourages prior-driven reason ing: agents form conclusions based on internal knowledge and use retrieval mainly to confirm them, resulting in confirma tion bias and inefficient evidenceuse.Toaddressthisissue, we propose Contextual Information Policy Optimization (CIPO), an evidence-oriented reinforcement learning framework that explicitly aligns policy optimization with external evidence use. CIPO assigns dense, turn-level credit to reasoning ac tions influenced by retrieved information, while combining this evidence-use signal with a global outcome reward to pre serveanswercorrectness.Withthismanner,CIPOdiscourages evidence-detached guesses and promotes reasoning trajecto ries in which retrieved facts can guide or revise subsequent reasoning. Importantly, CIPO requires neither human process annotations nor an additional reward model. Extensive exper iments on seven in-domain and out-of-domain benchmarks show that CIPO reduces the prevalence of prior-driven rea soning and achieves excellent performance on most tasks.

</details>


### [256] [FinEvo-Bench: A Longitudinal Benchmark for Self-Evolving Agents in Professional Financial Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06144)
*Bo Deng,Kang Zhou,Lifan Guo,Chongyang Tao,Xuanren Chen,Chenggang Xie,Renzhao Liang,Feng Chen,Chi Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出FinEvo-Bench，这是一个针对金融领域多场景、长周期任务的基准测试，用于评估智能体在专业工作流中的自我进化能力。通过对比四种自进化智能体架构，发现经验保留能显著提升任务质量并减少合规问题，其中Letta表现最佳，而技能进化优于纯记忆或混合进化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体基准测试大多独立评估任务，无法衡量经验对后续任务的帮助；现有的自我进化基准又未能同时涵盖专业工作流、开放式交付物和多维度评估。因此，需要一个新的基准来全面评估智能体在复杂专业场景下的长期自我进化能力。

Method: 构建FinEvo-Bench，包含120个基于真实案例的任务，覆盖6个金融领域的20个商业场景。每个场景由机构提供的专业程序定义操作约束，并由人工审核的评分标准评估质量和合规性。使用Qwen3.7-Max作为骨干模型，比较四种自进化智能体架构（包括Letta和Codex），设置非进化对照组以量化自我进化增益，并使用Claude Opus 4.6驱动的独立评分代理进行评估。

Result: 1. Letta获得最高进化得分（91.65）和最少的合规问题（每任务0.09个）；Codex实现最大的自我进化增益（+19.37）。
2. 在所有架构中，进化条件使得分提高9.33-19.37分，合规问题减少0.12-0.44个/任务。
3. 场景内排名4-6的任务得分增益高于排名1-3的任务（高出6.10-8.70分）。
4. 在Claude Code中，仅技能进化比仅记忆或记忆+技能进化产生更高的任务质量和更少的合规问题。
5. 所有四种架构中，基于评分标准的反馈（rubric feedback）优于参考答案反馈（reference-answer feedback）。

Conclusion: FinEvo-Bench有效衡量了智能体的专业表现和自我进化能力，证明了保留先前经验能显著提升智能体在复杂金融任务中的表现和合规性。研究还揭示了不同进化策略（如技能 vs 记忆）和反馈机制的效果差异，为设计更高效的自进化智能体提供了实证依据。

Abstract: Most agent benchmarks evaluate tasks independently and cannot measure whether experience from one task helps with later tasks. Existing self-evolution benchmarks do not jointly cover professional workflows, open-ended deliverables, and multi-aspect evaluation. We introduce FinEvo-Bench, a longitudinal benchmark with 120 real-case-grounded tasks, 20 business scenes across six financial domains. Institution-provided professional procedures define the required operations and constraints. Eligible institution-provided and publicly documented cases supply the task facts. Each scene contains six related but substantively distinct cases that share a professional procedure and a manually reviewed rubric for task quality and financial compliance. We compare four self-evolving agent scaffolds using the same Qwen3.7-Max backbone and three independently shuffled, globally interleaved task streams. Paired non-evolving controls estimate each scaffold's self-evolution gain from retained experience, while an independent Claude Code scoring agent backed by Claude Opus 4.6 evaluates all outputs. Letta achieves the highest evolved score (91.65) and fewest compliance issues (0.09 per task); Codex achieves the largest self-evolution gain (+19.37). Across scaffolds, the evolving condition raises scores by 9.33-19.37 points and reduces compliance issues by 0.12-0.44 per task. Paired score gains at within-scene ranks 4-6 exceed those at ranks 1-3 by 6.10-8.70 points. In Claude Code, skill-only evolution produces higher task quality and fewer compliance issues than memory-only and combined memory-skill evolution. Across all four scaffolds, rubric feedback also yields higher scores and fewer compliance issues than reference-answer feedback. FinEvo-Bench measures both professional performance and self-evolution ability: how effectively an agent turns prior experience into later improvement.

</details>


### [257] [PaDoc: Layout-Grounded Parallel Decoding for Document Parsing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06146)
*Hao Yu,Jiabo Zhan,Kang Liu,Linnan Zhao,Dongxu Yue,Rui Chen,Jinglin Wang,Chong Sun,Chen Li,Jing Lyu,Chun Yuan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出PaDoc，一种基于布局的端到端文档解析器，通过并行解码区域内容解决传统方法中序列长度与依赖问题，在OmniDocBench上取得顶尖性能，并在吞吐量（提升67.4-118%）和延迟（降低39.2-54.9%）方面显著优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有端到端解析器将页面布局和内容序列化为自回归序列，导致解码路径随内容增长而变长；基于裁剪的两阶段解析器虽能并行但牺牲了页面上下文完整性且需重复视觉预填充。旨在保留全页上下文的同时消除解码依赖。

Method: 提出PaDoc，将预测布局视为共享页面表示上的分支结构。利用区域充分性假设推导前缀条件因子分解，使布局流和区域内容并发推进。在单一多模态大语言模型（MLLM）中实现：使用打包变长祖先注意力保持标准训练下的可见性，并通过掩码并行解码创建分支，由vLLM后端作为并发请求服务并利用共享前缀缓存复用。

Result: 在OmniDocBench Full上，整体布局F1为91.1，端到端解析器综合得分达94.24（最高），文本编辑距离0.038，公式CDM 95.59。在384页子集和单张A800 GPU上，作为最快的端到端解析器，在不同并发级别下有效页面吞吐量提升67.4-118%，P95延迟降低39.2-54.9%。

Conclusion: PaDoc成功结合了端到端的上下文优势与两阶段方法的并行效率，实现了高性能和高吞吐量的文档解析，代码已开源。

Abstract: End-to-end document parsers provide a unified interface, but serialize page layouts and regional contents into one autoregressive sequence. This formulation forces independent regions onto a decoding path whose length grows with the total content, whereas crop-based two-stage parsers expose region-level parallelism at the cost of repeated visual prefills and fragmented page context. To retain full-page context while removing dependencies, we propose PaDoc, a layout-grounded parser that treats the predicted layout as a branching structure over a shared page representation. Under a region-sufficiency assumption, we derive a prefix-conditioned factorization in which the layout stream and regional content branches advance concurrently, reducing the decoding depth to the longest layout-content path. We realize this factorization within a single MLLM: packed variable-length ancestor attention preserves the visibility under standard next-token training, while masked parallel decoding creates branches that the evaluated vLLM backend serves as concurrent requests with cache-resident shared-prefix reuse. On OmniDocBench Full, PaDoc attains an Overall layout F1 of 91.1 and, among end-to-end parsers, a top-tier Overall score of 94.24 together with the best Text Edit (0.038) and Formula CDM (95.59). On a 384-page subset and one A800 GPU, it is the fastest end-to-end parser at five concurrency levels, improving valid-page throughput by 67.4-118% and reducing P95 latency by 39.2-54.9% relative to a same-backbone Sequential SFT baseline. Code is available at https://github.com/Longin-Yu/Padoc

</details>


### [258] [CogVis: Must Open-Vocabulary Change Detection Perceive the Scene Anew for Every Query?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06150)
*Zijie Wang,Chen Zhong,Wei He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出CogVis框架，将开放词汇变化检测重构为“感知-记忆-验证”范式，解耦时间证据与语义决策，实现SOTA性能并提升28.50%推理速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有开放词汇变化检测（OVCD）方法常混淆时间感知、语义判别和区域验证，导致结果不稳定且计算冗余。受人类视觉变化感知启发，旨在解决这些问题。

Method: 提出CogVis框架：1) 场景变化感知器（SCP）从冻结的双时相特征中提取可复用、类别无关的变化先验；2) 语义记忆校准器（SMC）动态估计图像-查询特定的决策阈值以补偿分数偏移；3) 自适应区域过滤器（ARF）利用学习到的语义、时间和结构可靠性过滤候选区域。

Result: 在涵盖语义变化检测、二值变化定位和建筑损害评估的七个基准测试上达到最先进（SOTA）性能；通过共享场景级变化感知，避免重复计算，推理吞吐量提升28.50%。

Conclusion: CogVis通过解耦时间感知与语义决策，有效提升了开放词汇变化检测的稳定性、准确性和效率。

Abstract: Earth-surface monitoring requires change detection models capable of recognizing arbitrary semantic categories. Open-Vocabulary Change Detection (OVCD) addresses this need. However, existing methods often entangle temporal perception, semantic discrimination, and region verification, causing unstable results and redundant computation. Inspired by human visual change perception, we propose CogVis, a cognitive memory-guided framework that reformulates OVCD as a perception-memory-verification paradigm. CogVis first employs a Scene Change Perceptron (SCP) to extract a reusable, category-agnostic change prior from frozen bi-temporal features, thereby decoupling temporal evidence from semantic category decisions. A Semantic Memory Calibrator (SMC) then compensates for category-dependent score shifts by dynamically estimating an image-query-specific decision threshold. Finally, an Adaptive Region Filter (ARF) filters connected candidates using learned semantic, temporal, and structural reliability. Experiments on seven benchmarks spanning semantic change detection, binary change localization, and building-damage assessment show that CogVis achieves state-of-the-art performance across all evaluated datasets. By sharing scene-level change perception, CogVis further avoids repeating category-agnostic temporal perception across queries and improves inference throughput by 28.50%.

</details>


### [259] [iARCS: Iterative Agentic RL for Controllable 3D Scene Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06161)
*Saugat Adhikari,Ashok Prasad Neupane,Pramish Paudel,Ajad Chhatkuli,Danda Pani Paudel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出iARCS框架，通过迭代代理强化学习将预训练场景生成器适配于自然语言任务需求，解决现有合成数据缺乏功能性约束（如可达性、可通行性）的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D场景生成器过度优化感知真实感，却未能可靠满足任务关键的功能性约束（如可访问性、可通行性和空间规则），限制了合成数据在下游训练中的实用性。

Method: 采用两阶段策略：首先进行通用奖励预以提升物理合理性和布局质量；随后利用LLM生成的奖励程序进行特定任务的微调，并通过训练反馈迭代优化这些奖励程序。

Result: 在步行、可达性和净空等任务上显著提高了约束遵循度，实现了有效的特定任务约束优化，并保持竞争力的场景多样性；生成的数据还能提升基础生成器的性能。

Conclusion: iARCS不仅是一种可控的场景编辑方法，更是一个实用的合成数据生成工具，能有效弥合感知真实感与功能性约束之间的差距。

Abstract: Synthetic 3D scene generation is increasingly used as a data source for computer vision and embodied AI, but existing generators often optimize perceptual realism without reliably satisfying task-critical functional constraints. This mismatch limits the usefulness of synthetic data for downstream training, where accessibility, traversability, and spatial rule compliance are often essential. We present iARCS, an iterative agentic reinforcement learning framework that adapts a pretrained scene generator to natural-language task requirements. iARCS uses a two-stage strategy: universal-reward pretraining to improve physical plausibility and layout quality, followed by task-specific fine-tuning with LLM-generated reward programs that are iteratively refined from training feedback. Experiments show improved constraint fidelity on walkability, reachability, and clearance-focused tasks, effective task-specific constraint optimization, and competitive scene diversity. We further show that data generated by iARCS improves a base generator, supporting its value as a practical synthetic data generation tool rather than only a controllable scene editing method.

</details>


### [260] [MicroEvo: Knowledge-Guided LLM Sampling for Efficient Microarchitecture Design Space Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06183)
*Jia Xiong,Runkai Li,Chenxu Niu,Guangyuan Gao,Changwen Xing,Yifan Zhang,Xinlai Wan,Jieran Cui,Chen Bai,Yusheng Hua,Ying Wang,Ming Ling,Xi Wang,Tao Xie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出MicroEvo框架，结合大语言模型（LLM）与蒙特卡洛树搜索（MCTS），解决微架构设计中搜索空间庞大及PPA评估昂贵的问题。通过知识引导、帕累托感知策略和主动知识积累机制，显著提升了优化质量和搜索效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有微架构设计空间探索方法存在搜索空间巨大、PPA（性能、功耗、面积）评估成本高昂的问题。传统方法往往进行盲目搜索，忽视微架构依赖性，且无法从迭代搜索中有效学习，导致评估浪费和帕累托收敛效果差。

Method: 提出MicroEvo框架，耦合现成LLM与MCTS进行多目标微架构优化。核心组件包括：1) LLM驱动的进化算子；2) 平衡帕累托贡献与多样性的帕累托感知树策略；3) 提取并复用优化洞察的主动知识积累机制；4) 在线适应搜索行为的感知状态指令。

Result: 实验表明，MicroEvo在帕累托前沿质量上比NSGA-II提升高达36.2%，搜索效率提高10.6倍，并在复杂工业级核心上展现出强大的可扩展性。

Conclusion: MicroEvo通过结合LLM的知识生成能力与MCTS的结构化搜索优势，有效解决了微架构设计中的效率与收敛性问题，为复杂芯片设计的自动化探索提供了高效解决方案。

Abstract: Microarchitecture design space exploration suffers from expansive search spaces and expensive PPA evaluation, leaving only a small simulation budget for design decision-making. Existing methods perform blind search without considering microarchitectural dependencies and fail to learn from the iterative search effectively, leading to wasted evaluations and weak Pareto convergence. In this paper, we propose MicroEvo, a knowledge-guided framework that couples off-the-shelf LLMs with Monte Carlo Tree Search (MCTS) for multi-objective microarchitecture optimization. MicroEvo combines LLM-driven evolutionary operators, a Pareto-aware tree policy that balances Pareto contribution and diversity, an active knowledge accumulation mechanism that extracts and reuses optimization insights, and state-aware directives that adapt the search behavior online. Experiments show that MicroEvo improves Pareto-front quality by up to 36.2% over NSGA-II and achieves 10.6x higher search efficiency, and also demonstrates strong scalability to a complex industrial-scale core. The code repository is available at: https://github.com/GEAR-SEU/MicroEvo-ICCAD-26.

</details>


### [261] [Comparative Approaches to Agent Retrieval over Large Skill Libraries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06196)
*Indivara Kolluru,Nathan Sportsman*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究在大型技能库中如何高效加载和排序技能。对比了混合检索器（词汇+密集嵌入）与类型化知识图谱两种方法。结果显示，混合检索器表现优异（Top-5命中率达73.5%），而知识图谱并未提升性能，反而因拓扑限制无法扩展检索范围。作者指出，强检索器已覆盖大部分相关技能，结构化的图关系仅能丰富语义而无法扩大召回率，且使用作者编写查询会严重高估模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决拥有大型技能库的智能体在决定加载哪些技能及加载顺序时面临的挑战。直接加载整个库成本高昂且缺乏自主序列化的结构支持，因此需要高效的检索和排序机制。

Method: 1. 数据集：690个技能的语料库，117个真实的非回声查询测试集。
2. 系统A：混合排名器（Hybrid Ranker），结合词汇检索和密集嵌入检索，用于稀疏、按需加载。
3. 系统B：类型化知识图谱（Typed Knowledge Graph），编码工作流关系（如先决条件、数据流、顺序）。
4. 评估指标：Hit@5（前5名命中率），并分析LLM生成的边层与本地嵌入邻居的效果差异。

Result: 1. 混合排名器在117个查询中，73.5% +/- 8.0的情况下能将正确技能检索至前5名。
2. 知识图谱作为设计意图的补充（用图邻居替换额外排名结果以匹配Token预算）时，性能显著下降（降低11.2分，p=0.0007）。
3. LLM生成的边层相比本地嵌入获得的邻居没有额外优势；73%被排名器遗漏的查询在图中完全不可达。
4. 归因于“预过滤拓扑绑定”：98.6%的类型化边连接的技能已被排名器共同发现，图无法扩展检索可达性。
5. 使用作者编写的查询评估会将Hit@5高估多达44分，从而掩盖上述缺陷。

Conclusion: 增加结构化依赖关系并不总能提高对强检索器的检索效果。当检索器已经足够强大时，额外的图结构仅能丰富关系语义，而无法扩展检索范围。只有在特定条件下，将结构性相互依赖性引入检索才是最优的。此外，评估方法（如使用作者查询）可能严重误导性能判断。

Abstract: Agents backed by large skill libraries must decide which skills to load and in what order. Loading the entire library into context is expensive and provides no structure for autonomous sequencing. We study two systems for this problem over a corpus of 690 skills: a hybrid ranker combining lexical and dense-embedding retrieval for sparse, on-demand loading, and a typed knowledge graph encoding workflow relations such as prerequisites, data flow, and ordering. On a set of 117 realistic, non-echoing queries, the hybrid ranker retrieves the correct skill within the top five in 73.5% +/- 8.0 of cases, leaving roughly a quarter of queries unserved. When used as the design intended (substituting graph neighbours for additional ranked results at matched token budget), the graph is significantly worse (-11.2 points, p = 0.0007). Its LLM-generated edge layer adds nothing over neighbours obtained free from a local embedding pass, and 73% of the queries the ranker misses are not reachable through the graph at all. We attribute this to a pre-filter topology bound. Because the graph's candidate edges are drawn from the same embedding neighbourhood the ranker already searches, 98.6% of typed edges connect skills the ranker had already surfaced together. The graph can enrich relation semantics but cannot extend retrieval reach. We further show that evaluating on author-written queries overstates hit@5 by up to 44 points, which would have hidden these results entirely. Our contribution is a mechanistic account of why added structure does not improve retrieval over a strong ranker, and identify the conditions under which adding structural interdependence into the retrieval is optimal.

</details>


### [262] [EnvACE: Internalizing Environment Dynamics via World Rehearsal for Agentic Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06197)
*Zishan Xu,Zhiyuan Yao,Yuxin Chen,Yifu Guo,Zhengxi Lu,Yuquan Lu,Jinyang Huang,Yan Xu,Yasheng Wang,Weinan Zhang,Xingshan Zeng,Weiwen Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出EnvACE方法，通过“世界预演”替代昂贵的真实/合成环境交互来训练LLM智能体。策略在“执行”和“扮演环境生成反馈”之间交替，联合优化以内化动作与环境响应的关系。该方法在多个基准测试中表现优异，并在测试时支持私有预演以提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长周期工具使用的大语言模型（LLM）智能体训练依赖高昂的真实或合成可执行环境构建与验证，或依赖难以接地（grounding）的外部模拟器，存在成本高且实施困难的问题。

Method: 引入EnvACE，一种基于代理强化学习的方法。核心机制为“世界预演”（world rehearsal）：策略交替进行行动生成和环境角色模拟（生成该行动诱导的响应），并将后续决策条件化于预演的响应上。通过任务成功奖励对两个角色进行端到端的联合优化，使策略内部化动作与环境响应之间的关系，形成直接支持决策的智能体世界模型。

Result: 在BFCL-v4、tau^2-Bench、VitaBench和FinMCP-Bench等多个基准测试中，EnvACE实现了强大且可迁移的性能，整体评估优于环境扩展基线。控制实验表明，世界预演在不同模型规模下均能持续改善策略学习。测试阶段，内部化的世界模型允许在承诺执行前进行私有预演，在不增加外部交互的情况下，通过适度的预演预算进一步提升了性能。

Conclusion: 世界预演为突破外部环境约束、扩展LLM智能体训练提供了新的路径。该方法不仅提高了训练效率和性能，还展示了良好的可扩展性和迁移能力。

Abstract: Training large language model agents for long-horizon tool use typically relies on interactions with real or synthesized executable environments, whose construction and verification are costly, or on external simulators that are difficult to ground. We introduce EnvACE, an agentic reinforcement learning method that replaces external environment interaction during training with world rehearsal. The policy alternates between acting and rehearsal: it first generates a tool call, then plays the role of the environment to produce the response induced by that action, and conditions subsequent decisions on the rehearsed response. Both roles are jointly optimized end-to-end using task-success rewards. Through world rehearsal, the policy internalizes the relationship between actions and their environment responses in its parameters, yielding an agent world model that directly supports decision making. Across BFCL-v4, tau^2-Bench, VitaBench, and FinMCP-Bench, EnvACE achieves strong and transferable performance, outperforming environment-scaling baselines in the overall evaluation. Controlled studies further show that world rehearsal consistently improves policy learning across model scales. At test time, the internalized world model enables private rehearsal before committed execution, yielding further gains under a moderate rehearsal budget without additional external interaction. Our findings establish world rehearsal as a new path toward scaling LLM agent training beyond the constraints of external environments. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/Within-yao/EnvACE.

</details>


### [263] [TS-RAG: Retrieval Augmented Generation for Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06223)
*Yixiong Xiao,Congxi Xiao,Jingbo Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出TS-RAG框架，将检索增强生成（RAG）应用于时间序列预测，通过专用参考token融合输入与检索到的相似序列信息，解决传统方法简单拼接效果不佳的问题，并在多个基准测试中实现最先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管Transformer模型在时间序列预测中表现优异，但RAG在该领域的应用有限。由于时间序列模型通常受限于训练数据少、参数量小且缺乏大语言模型的强大生成能力，直接复制语言模型中将参考序列拼接至提示词的做法难以奏效，因此需要一种专门针对时间序列特性的RAG方法来提升预测精度。

Method: 提出TS-RAG框架，利用RAG技术增强预测性能。该方法设计了专门的参考token（reference tokens），用于有效融合输入序列信息与检索到的相似时间序列参考信息，从而更稳健地捕捉复杂的时序动态特征。

Result: 实验结果表明，TS-RAG在多个真实世界的时间序列预测基准测试中，均取得了持续的最先进（state-of-the-art）性能。

Conclusion: TS-RAG通过引入专为时间序列设计的参考机制，成功解决了RAG在时间序列应用中面临的挑战，显著提升了预测准确性，证明了检索增强技术在时间序列领域的有效性。

Abstract: While deep learning models, particularly transformer-based architectures, have shown impressive performance in time series forecasting, the application of retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) in this domain remains limited. Since RAG has proven effective in enhancing the capabilities of large language models by incorporating relevant external information, retrieving similar time series sequences as references might also improve accuracy in time series forecasting tasks. However, most time series models are constrained by limited training data, smaller parameter scales, and a lack of the extensive generative capabilities found in large language models. Simply concatenating reference sequences into the prompt, as done in language models, may not yield the expected results. To address these challenges, we propose a novel approach, TS-RAG, which leverages RAG to enhance forecasting performance. The framework introduces specially designed reference tokens to effectively fuse information from the input sequence with that from retrieved similar sequences, enabling a more robust capture of complex temporal dynamics. Experimental results demonstrate that TS-RAG achieves consistent state-of-the-art performance across several real-world forecasting benchmarks.

</details>


### [264] [DASH: Divergence-Adaptive Supervision Horizons for On-Policy Self-Distillation of Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06243)
*ZhiYan Hou,Xinyu Tang,Hongyan An,Jianjin Zhang,Weizhen Wang,Yunyun Han,Gengsheng Li,Xiangzhao Hao,Haiyun Guo,Wenbin Hu,Jinqiao Wang,Yafeng Deng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出DASH方法，通过自适应传播门控机制解决OPSD中忽略时间结构的问题，利用局部散度与序列均值的差距动态调整token级监督权重，在数学推理任务上显著提升性能且无需额外计算开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有强化学习可验证奖励（RLVR）信号稀疏；On-policy自蒸馏（OPSD）虽提供密集token级监督，但未能充分利用rollout的时间结构，对所有局部差异赋予相同系数，无法区分不同演化路径下的不匹配历史。

Method: 提出发散适应性监督视界（DASH）。该方法将每个局部蒸馏信号与序列级均值的差距映射为自适应传播门控，并利用这些门控控制反向多步聚合，从而根据生成过程中局部发散的演化情况动态调整token级监督权重。

Result: 在三个模型规模、三个数学推理基准上的实验表明，DASH在所有设置下均优于基线vanilla OPSD。由于复用已有的教师和学生分布，该方法在提升性能的同时无需额外的前向传播计算成本。

Conclusion: DASH通过引入对时间结构和发散演化的感知，有效克服了标准OPSD的局限性，实现了更高效、更精准的密集监督，证明了自适应权重分配在大模型推理能力增强中的有效性。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) improves the reasoning capabilities of large language models using automatically verifiable outcome signals, but these signals are typically sparse and at the sequence-level. On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) mitigates this sparsity by querying a privileged teacher at student-visited prefixes and providing dense token-level distributional supervision. Although this dense supervision alleviates signal sparsity, we find that standard OPSD still underexploits the temporal structure of the rollout. It assigns every local divergence the same coefficient, regardless of its position or the divergence sequence in which it occurs. In on-policy autoregressive generation, the same divergence magnitude can follow different discrepancy histories, reflecting different evolutions of the mismatch between the teacher and student. Since the local scalar alone cannot distinguish these temporal contexts, standard OPSD cannot adapt its token-level weights to the realized discrepancy sequence. To address this limitation, we propose Divergence-Adaptive Supervision Horizons (DASH). DASH maps the gap between each local distillation signal and the sequence-level mean to an adaptive propagation gate and then uses these gates to control backward multi-step aggregation. By doing so, DASH adjusts token-level supervision weights according to how local divergences evolve during generation. Experiments on three mathematical reasoning benchmarks across three model scales show that DASH improves over our matched vanilla OPSD reruns on every benchmark at all three scales. DASH reuses the teacher and student distributions that OPSD already computes, so the gains require no additional teacher or student forward pass.
  Code: https://github.com/DBtxy/DASH-OPSD

</details>


### [265] [The Illusion of Visual Tool-Use: A Causal Audit of Thinking with Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06270)
*Zhiheng Wang,Bo Peng,Lai Wei,Chaochao Lu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究通过因果干预审计发现，当前多模态大模型（MLLMs）的“图像思维”范式存在“视觉工具使用幻觉”。尽管整体准确率有所提升，但这种提升主要集中在少数校准良好的样本上；在大多数情况下，返回的视觉证据对答案没有因果影响（叫而不看），或者调用策略混乱（看而无计划）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的具备主动视觉操作（如裁剪、缩放）的多模态LLMs虽然成本高昂，但相比直接推理仅带来边际甚至负面的收益，且常出现重复裁剪无关区域或在直接推理能正确回答的问题上失败的情况。研究者旨在探究返回的视觉证据是否真正因果性地影响了最终答案。

Method: 将视觉工具使用形式化为因果图，区分观察中介路径和行动诱导捷径。通过在三个层级进行干预来审计：1) 策略层（比较工具使用与直接推理）；2) 轨迹层（在 rollout 过程中破坏所有观察结果）；3) 步骤层（在固定前缀下反事实替换单个观察结果）。提出“视觉证据增益”作为步骤级估计量，以隔离每个返回观察结果的贡献。

Result: 在六个代表性模型和五个细粒度感知基准测试中发现两种政策失准模式：“叫而不看”（Calling Without Looking，返回的观察结果对答案无因果影响）和“看而无计划”（Looking Without Planning，观察结果虽具信息量但调用顺序不连贯）。轨迹层诊断显示，准确率的提升集中在少数“校准良好”的样本中，而非广泛有效。

Conclusion: 揭示了“视觉工具使用幻觉”现象：尽管聚合准确率有所提升，但在广泛的 rollout 中，视觉工具使用并非因果有效。研究代码已开源。

Abstract: The "thinking-with-images" paradigm equips multimodal LLMs with active visual operations such as crop-and-zoom. However, models using these operations often achieve only marginal or negative gains over direct inference at substantially higher token cost. They may also repeatedly crop irrelevant regions and fail on questions that direct inference answers correctly. We ask whether the returned visual evidence causally affects the answer. To answer this question, we formulate visual tool-use as a causal graph that separates observation-mediated paths from action-induced shortcuts. We then audit it through interventions at the three levels: policy (comparing tool-use with direct inference), trajectory (corrupting all observations during rollout), and step (counterfactually replacing one individual observation under a fixed prefix). Our step-level estimand, Visual Evidence Gain, isolates the contribution of each returned observation. Across six representative models and five fine-grained perception benchmarks, we uncover policy miscalibration with two failure modes. In Calling Without Looking, returned observations have no causal effect on the answer. In Looking Without Planning, observations are informative but the call schedule is incoherent. A trajectory-level diagnostic decomposes the policy-level accuracy gain and shows that the gain is concentrated in a Calibrated minority. We term this discrepancy the illusion of visual tool-use: despite aggregate accuracy gains, visual tool-use is not causally effective across a broad range of rollouts. The code is available at https://github.com/OpenCausaLab/CauAudit.

</details>


### [266] [QuanTiMedAI: Quantum-Enhanced Time-Series Model guided by Agentic AI for Cardiac Arrest Mortality Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06294)
*Mutasim Fuad Sarker,Adiba Rahman Namira,Wafa Binte Alam,Md Adnan Arefeen,Mahzabeen Emu,Sumaiya Tabassum Nimi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出QuanTiMedAI框架，结合Agentic LLM进行特征发现与量子循环网络进行时序建模，在MIMIC-IV心脏骤停患者数据上实现0.852 AUROC，仅需605参数，性能优于现有SOTA基线约2.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ICU心脏骤停死亡率预测研究多依赖入院早期的静态摘要，忽略了患者在住院期间生理状态随时间恶化或恢复的动态时序变化，导致预测能力受限。

Method: 构建QuanTiMedAI量子智能体框架：1) 利用Agentic大语言模型（LLM）进行临床 informed 的特征发现与选择；2) 采用紧凑型量子循环网络进行感知时序的死亡率预测，通过非线性特征增强提升预测性能。

Result: 在MIMIC-IV队列实验中，QuanTiMedAI以仅605个参数的极低参数量，达到0.852的AUROC指标，较当前该任务的最先进基线提升约2.9%。消融实验验证了各架构设计的有效性，证明量子增强序列建模在极少参数下可超越经典循环网络。

Conclusion: 结合Agentic LLM引导的特征选择与轻量级量子循环网络，能有效捕捉生理状态的时序动态，显著提升心脏骤停死亡率预测精度，同时大幅降低模型复杂度。

Abstract: Cardiac arrest remains one of the most lethal conditions encountered in intensive care units. Despite the growing availability of electronic health record data, existing mortality prediction studies in this population largely depend on static summaries derived from early admission. Such approaches ignore the temporal progression of physiological deterioration and recovery that unfolds throughout a patient's ICU stay. To address this limitation, we introduce QuanTiMedAI, a quantum-agentic framework developed for cardiac arrest mortality prediction using agentic AI guided quantum enhancement time series model. The proposed system combines an agentic large language model (LLM) for clinically informed feature discovery with a compact quantum recurrent network for temporality aware mortality prediction. Our findings demonstrate that agentic LLM-guided feature selection consistently outperforms conventional feature selection approaches, and the proposed quantum architecture achieves competitive predictive performance through nonlinear feature enhancement while keeping the number of parameters very low. Through extensive experimentation on a MIMIC-IV cohort of cardiac arrest patients, QuanTiMedAI's quantum-enhanced architecture attains an AUROC of 0.852 using only 605 parameters, an improvement of approximately 2.9\% over a current state-of-the-art baseline for this task. A structured ablation study systematically validates the contribution of each architectural design choice. These results show that quantum-enhanced sequential modeling can exceed classical recurrent networks while using substantially fewer parameters.

</details>


### [267] [Bias Analysis of L2 Speaking Assessment Systems Using Concept Activation Vectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06300)
*Arya Labroo,Mengjie Qian,Kate Knill*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过扩展概念激活向量（CAVs）分析，评估了基于BERT和Whisper的自动口语评分系统对无关属性（如L1、年龄）的偏差。研究发现概念的可恢复性取决于模型架构而非概念本身；稀疏自编码器（SAEs）虽提高了线性可分性，但削弱了原始激活空间的敏感度。结论强调在审计偏差时需区分“概念可恢复性”与“概念影响力”。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着自动口语评分系统在高风险场景中的广泛应用，确保评分仅依赖语言 proficiency 而非无关属性（如第一语言或年龄）至关重要。尽管Transformer模型提高了评分准确率，但其黑盒特性使得公平性和可解释性分析变得困难。

Method: 1. 将基于CAVs的分析扩展到两个神经口语评分系统：基于文本的BERT评分器和基于Whisper的多模态评分器。
2. 使用CAV表示人类可解释的概念方向，并通过基于梯度的敏感性指标量化概念对预测分数的影响。
3. 引入稀疏自编码器（SAEs），在稀疏潜在空间中学习CAVs并映射回激活空间，以解决复杂神经网络嵌入空间中线性可分性较差的问题。

Result: 1. 概念的可恢复性强烈依赖于被探测的表示和架构，而非概念本身。
2. 对概念的敏感性也具有架构依赖性。
3. SAEs使概念更具线性可分性，但在低维层中会减弱原始激活空间的敏感度。

Conclusion: 在审计口语评分系统的偏差时，必须严格区分“概念可恢复性”（概念是否被编码）与“概念影响力”（概念是否影响最终评分）。单纯提高概念的可检测性并不等同于消除了模型对该概念的依赖或偏见。

Abstract: Automatic speaking assessment systems are increasingly deployed in high-stakes settings to mark second language (L2) learners' speaking tests, making it critical to show that their scores depend on speaking proficiency rather than irrelevant speaker attributes such as first language (L1) or age. Transformer-based foundation models have improved the accuracy of these L2 speaking graders, but their black-box representations make fairness and interpretability analysis more difficult. Building on prior work that used Concept Activation Vectors (CAVs) to detect bias towards unwanted attributes (`concepts') in feature-based graders, we extend CAV-based analysis to two neural speaking assessment systems: a text-based BERT grader and a speech-and-text multimodal grader based on Whisper. CAVs represent human-interpretable concepts as directions in a model's activation space, allowing us to distinguish between whether a concept is encoded in a model's internal representations and whether it influences the predicted score, the latter quantified using a gradient-based sensitivity metric. Since CAVs rely on linear separability, which is less likely in complex neural embedding spaces, we also investigate whether sparse autoencoders (SAEs) provide cleaner concept directions by learning CAVs in a sparse latent space and mapping them back to activation space. Our analysis shows that concept recoverability depends strongly on the representation and architecture being probed, rather than on the concept alone. Sensitivity to concepts is also architecture-dependent. SAEs make concepts more linearly recoverable, but attenuate the original activation-space sensitivity, especially in low-dimensional layers. These findings highlight the need to distinguish concept recoverability from concept influence when auditing bias in speaking assessment systems.

</details>


### [268] [TRAJDEBUG: Tracing Error Lifecycle to Identify Critical Failures in Long-Horizon Agent Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06346)
*Yunjia Qi,Zehua Yin,Xintong Shi,Hao Peng,Songyuanyi Lu,Yixian Liu,Richeng Xuan,Yuhong Liu,Zhichao Hu,Xiaozhi Wang,Lei Hou,Bin Xu,Juanzi Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出TrajDebug框架，通过多粒度历史压缩和基于证据的错误识别，解决LLM智能体长轨迹中的关键错误检测与归因问题；构建TrajErrBench基准测试，实验表明该方法性能优于现有基线，能提供可操作的反馈以提升智能体成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM驱动的智能体系统在复杂领域表现优异，但面临级联错误和调试困难的问题。现有的关键错误检测旨在定位导致最终失败的最早错误步骤，但受限于长轨迹中证据分散以及多个局部错误对最终影响不同的挑战，难以准确识别。

Method: 提出TrajDebug框架，包含多粒度历史压缩和基于证据的错误识别技术，以解决长轨迹错误发现难题；支持通过追踪每个错误的解决状态和终端影响来进行关键归因。同时构建TrajErrBench基准，包含来自Tau2Bench和SWE-Bench Pro的486条人工标注失败轨迹，涵盖真实工具使用和编码场景。

Result: 在多种智能体基准测试上，TrajDebug的整体性能优于现有基线方法。应用研究进一步证明，其诊断结果能为提升下游智能体的成功率提供可操作的反馈。代码和数据将开源。

Conclusion: TrajDebug有效解决了长轨迹中的错误检测与归因挑战，为改进LLM智能体的可靠性和可调试性提供了有力工具，并通过开源促进后续研究。

Abstract: LLM-based agentic systems have shown remarkable capabilities in complex domains, while suffering from cascading errors and difficulty in debugging. Critical error detection aims to locate the earliest error step in a failed trajectory that is responsible for the final failure. However, progress faces two main challenges. First, long trajectories make it difficult to identify individual errors, since the evidence for judging a step may be scattered across distant instructions, observations, and prior context. Second, failed trajectories often contain multiple local errors with different downstream effects, only some of which remain responsible for the final failure. In this work, we propose TrajDebug, an error-lifecycle tracing framework that addresses long-trajectory error discovery with multi-granularity history compression and evidence-based error identification, and supports critical attribution by tracing each error's resolution status and terminal impact. We further construct TrajErrBench, a benchmark of 486 manually annotated failed trajectories from Tau2Bench and SWE-Bench Pro, covering realistic tool-use and coding scenarios. Experiments across diverse agent benchmarks show that TrajDebug achieves the best overall performance over existing baselines, and application studies further demonstrate that its diagnoses provide actionable feedback for improving downstream agent success. We will release the codes and data to facilitate further research.

</details>


### [269] [Challenges in Evaluating Explanation Methods for Static and Evolving Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06351)
*Jerzy Stefanowski*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文探讨了可解释人工智能（XAI）在评估方面的局限性，通过DetoxAI系统展示偏见检测与概念遗忘的应用，介绍了面向图像分类的人本评估方法，讨论了应对概念漂移的解释适应性策略（如反事实解释），并分析了数据、模型与解释协同演进的追踪挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对当前可解释人工智能（XAI）领域存在的评估不足问题，旨在通过具体案例和方法论探讨，提升XAI系统在动态环境下的可靠性与可解释性评估能力。

Method: 1. 利用DetoxAI图像识别系统进行偏见检测和概念遗忘的实证分析；2. 开展面向图像分类解释方法的人本评估（human-grounded evaluation）；3. 探索适应概念漂移的数据流解释调整方法，重点讨论反事实解释的适应性应用；4. 分析数据、模型与解释三者协同演进的追踪机制。

Result: 揭示了现有XAI评估体系的不足，展示了人本评估和反事实解释在应对概念漂移中的有效性，并提出了跟踪数据-模型-解释协同演进的框架思路。

Conclusion: XAI研究需加强评估维度的系统性构建，特别是在动态数据环境下，应重视人本评估与适应性解释技术，以实现对模型行为及其演变的持续、可靠监控。

Abstract: This paper addresses the limitations of Explainable Artificial Intelligence (XAI) with respect to insufficient evaluation. They are illustrated through the DetoxAI image recognition system for bias detection and concept unlearning. Then, an example of a human-grounded evaluation of methods for explaining image classification is presented. The paper further explores methods for adapting explanations to evolving data streams with concept drift. Experiences with adapting counterfactuals for this problem are discussed. Finally it is related to the challenges of tracking the co-evolution of data, models, and explanations.\footnote{This paper has been accepted for a publication in J.Nalepa (ed) Explainable AI in Space. Proceedings of EASi 2026 Workshop at IJCAI-ECAI 2026 Bremen, Springer CCIS vol 3107 (2016).}

</details>


### [270] [The Low Frequency Trap: Video Language Models Fail at Simple Event Bookkeeping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06361)
*Sarvesh Baskar,Zikui Cai,Shayan Shabihi,Anirudh Satheesh,Muhammad R. Islam,Udari Madhushani Sehwag,Tom Goldstein,Furong Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于可执行轨迹的参数化分析方法，用于评估视频事件计数的能力。研究发现，当前模型在高频、高数量事件下的表现极差，且增加帧率或调整提示策略无法根本解决时序推理失败的问题，揭示了从聚合准确率向详细诊断性评估转变的必要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界视频基准虽然覆盖广泛，但其固定片段混淆了事件数量、频率、持续时间和视觉复杂度，导致难以隔离模型的失败模式；而现有的程序化基准仅对最终答案评分，缺乏对报告事件与可执行真实值（ground truth）之间的一致性审计。

Method: 引入了“基于轨迹的参数化分析”方法，在三个受控视频任务中（弹球墙壁接触、视觉眨眼、分类状态转换）进行实验。通过2,190个视频，独立变化事件数量N和频率F，同时保持渲染固定。每个视频包含可执行的事件轨迹，用于能力表面估计和时间戳级别的评估。

Result: 1. 发现阶段性的时序失败：在80%可靠性阈值下，Gemini 3.6 Flash能可靠计数最多12个持久状态转换（0.5-1.0 Hz），但对瞬态眨眼事件无可靠计数区域。
2. 事件表征决定证据访问：在高数量、高频率 regime 下，仅0.2%的最终计数正确，模型仅恢复18.1%的真实事件。
3. 额外帧数无效：提高采样率虽使弹球任务准确率从19.6%升至29.3%，但报告序列与真实值一致率仅3.7%，表明额外帧数可能虚增分数而非提升忠实的事件恢复。
4. 提示策略效果有限：不同提示策略增益相似，真实世界视频评估也显示成功集中在低事件数量。

Conclusion: 事件表征决定了模型是否能初始访问证据，这一限制随数量和频率增加而加剧。单纯增加视觉输入（帧数）或调整提示无法解决根本问题。基于轨迹的分析将视频评估从聚合准确率指标转变为对时序推理失败位置的详细诊断。

Abstract: Real-world video benchmarks provide broad coverage, but their fixed clips entangle event count, rate, duration, and visual complexity, making failure modes hard to isolate. While existing programmatic benchmarks offer better control, they score only the final answer rather than auditing reported events against executable ground truth. To bridge this gap, we introduce trace-grounded parametric profiling for event counting in three controlled video tasks: bouncing-ball wall contacts, visual blinks, and categorical state transitions. Across 2,190 videos, we vary event count N and frequency F while holding rendering fixed. Each video includes an executable event trace for capability-surface estimation and timestamp-level evaluation. Our results reveal a staged temporal failure. At an 80% reliability threshold, Gemini 3.6 Flash reliably counts persistent state transitions up to 12 events at 0.5 and 1.0 Hz, yet demonstrates no reliable positive-count region for transient blinking events. Thus, event representation dictates whether a model initially accesses evidence -- a limitation that compounds as count and frequency increase. In the high-count, high-frequency regime, only 0.2% of final counts are correct and the model recovers just 18.1% of true events. To test if visual access is the primary bottleneck, we increase sampling rate. Although this boosts Bounce Ball accuracy from 19.6% to 29.3%, the reported sequence agrees with ground truth only 3.7% of the time. Extra frames can therefore inflate final scores without producing faithful event recovery. Different prompting strategies yield similarly limited gains, and real-world video evaluations show the same concentration of success at low event counts. Ultimately, trace-grounded profiling shifts video evaluation from aggregate accuracy metrics to a detailed diagnostic of where temporal reasoning fails.

</details>


### [271] [Tracing the Heart: An Evidence-Linked Pipeline for Heart-Failure Feature Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06366)
*Soorya Ram Shimgekar,Michelle Hu,Dorisa Shehi,Daniel Kang,Roy Ka-Wei Lee,Koustuv Saha,Christian Poellabauer,Christopher Lee,Sajeev Singh,Piyum Zonooz,Navin Kumar,Zeeshan Ahmed,Priyadarshini Kachroo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为Nimblemind多智能体系统（nMAS）的自动化特征工程管道，专门用于心力衰竭患者的电子健康记录（EHR）数据处理。该系统通过证据链接和评分标准引导，成功生成了结构化及聚合特征，并在HFrEF和HFpEF表型分类任务中显著提升了模型性能（AUROC分别提升至0.963和0.910），证明了自动化、可审计的特征工程在复杂心血管数据中的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电子健康记录（EHR）特征工程是临床研究和AI领域的主要瓶颈，占数据科学家工作量的39-45%。在心衰研究中，需整合碎片化的EHR数据并结合基于指南的临床推理。现有的基于规则和大语言模型（LLM）的方法仅能提供部分自动化，且存在可维护性差和证据追溯性有限的问题。

Method: 开发了Nimblemind多智能体系统（nMAS），这是一个基于证据链接和评分标准的自动化心衰特征工程管道。研究使用来自9个EHR源表的500条虚拟患者记录进行评估。nMAS生成结构化特征和经评分标准加权的聚合特征，并通过受限LLM进行结构完整性、合规性及溯源性的验证与审计。

Result: nMAS生成了132个结构化特征和70个聚合特征。引入这些聚合特征后，HFrEF表型分类的保留集AUROC从0.895提升至0.963，HFpEF从0.870提升至0.910。独立LLM评估显示，生成的特征在证据支持和方法论严谨性方面获得了满分81.5%的分数。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了针对复杂心血管EHR数据进行自动化、可审计特征工程的可行性。然而，由于评估仅限于单中心队列，未来需要进行外部验证以确认其泛化能力。

Abstract: Electronic health record (EHR) feature engineering is a major bottleneck in clinical research and AI, accounting for 39-45% of data scientists' workload. This is especially pronounced in heart failure, which affects an estimated 6.7 million U.S. adults and requires integrating fragmented EHR data with disease-specific, guideline-based clinical reasoning. Existing rule-based and large language model (LLM)-based approaches offer only partial automation with limited maintainability and evidence traceability. We developed the Nimblemind Multi-Agent System (nMAS), an evidence-linked, rubric-grounded pipeline for automated heart-failure feature engineering, and evaluated it on 500 dummy patient records from nine EHR source tables. nMAS generated 132 structured and 70 rubric-scored aggregated features, verified for structural integrity, rubric compliance, and provenance, and audited by a restricted LLM. Adding the aggregated features improved held-out AUROC from 0.895 to 0.963 for HFrEF and 0.870 to 0.910 for HFpEF phenotyping, and an independent LLM-based rubric assessment of evidence support and methodological soundness scored the features at 81.5% of maximum points. These results demonstrate the feasibility of automated, auditable feature engineering for complex cardiovascular EHR data, though evaluation was limited to a single-institution cohort and external validation is needed.

</details>


<div id='cs.DB'></div>

# cs.DB [[Back]](#toc)

### [272] [Temporal and Conceptual Modeling: Foundations and Research Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05342)
*Nina Edelweiss,José Palazzo M. de Oliveira*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文分析了Nina Edelweiss和José Palazzo Moreira de Oliveira在1993至1997年间关于时序与概念建模的研究发展及其后续影响，展示了从TF-ORM框架到动态及基于知识的信息系统方法的连贯演进，并指出其对未来溯源、本体演化等领域的预见性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 梳理并评估Nina Edelweiss和José Palazzo Moreira de Oliveira早期时序与概念建模研究的演变过程，以及这些研究如何通过研究生指导（UFRGS）扩展为更广泛的动态和信息集成系统工作。

Method: 通过追溯1993年至1997年间的一系列研究（涉及对象建模扩展、时序数据类型、双时态查询等），分析其在UFRGS指导下对时序查询处理、索引、版本管理等领域的支撑作用，并将原始TF-ORM框架与后续研究轨迹相连接。

Result: 识别出从形式化时序数据库模型向更广泛的动态和基于知识的信息系统方法发展的连贯进程；确认了该系列研究在溯源、可追溯性、本体演化、知识图谱及语义一致自适应系统等当前热点领域的前瞻性贡献。

Conclusion: Nina Edelweiss和José Palazzo Moreira de Oliveira的早期建模研究不仅奠定了时序数据处理的基础，还预示了现代知识系统和自适应信息处理的关键需求，展现了学术传承与技术演进的连续性。

Abstract: This paper examines the development and subsequent influence of temporal and conceptual modeling research conducted by Nina Edelweiss and José Palazzo Moreira de Oliveira. It traces a sequence of studies from 1993 to 1997 that extended object-oriented modeling with temporal data types, historical states, bitemporal queries, multi-model integration, roles, and schema evolution. The analysis then considers graduate research supervised at UFRGS, showing how these foundations supported work on temporal query processing, indexing, version management, schema versioning, adaptive systems, information integration, and evolving knowledge. By connecting the original TF-ORM framework to subsequent research trajectories, the paper identifies a coherent progression from formal temporal database models to broader approaches to dynamic and knowledge-based information systems. These contributions anticipated current concerns in provenance, traceability, ontology evolution, knowledge graphs, and semantically consistent adaptive systems.

</details>


### [273] [BEGIN AI TRANSACTION: Semantic Isolation for Durable AI Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05412)
*Barzan Mozafari*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 针对AI工作流因环境动态变化导致的内部不一致问题，提出语义隔离（SemIso）框架。通过定义四种异常并建立隔离级别偏序关系，在中间件层实现高效检测与强制执行，确保工作流语义一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI执行过程日益复杂，涉及暂停、重试、分支及动态工具选择等长生命周期操作。由于提示词、模型别名、策略等资源独立部署且可能动态变更，导致工作流结合保存状态与新假设时产生内部不一致。现有数据库事务约束无法解决AI工作流语义环境的并发变更问题，亟需新的隔离机制。

Method: 1. 定义四种可自动检测的异常：语义读取偏差（semantic read skew）、兼容性偏差（compatibility skew）、上下文逃逸（context escape）和合并偏差（merge skew）。
2. 基于资源稳定性、跨资源兼容性和延续继承性三项保证，推导从“语义读已提交”到“语义快照隔离”的隔离级别偏序关系。
3. 开发原型系统SemIso，作为中间件传播语义上下文，并通过微秒级检查阻止不兼容资源和分支合并。
4. 对100个高星LangGraph仓库进行保守源码审计，评估现实代码中的相关风险。

Result: 1. 在100个包含持久化工作流的LangGraph代码库中，发现7.4%的代码在同一工作流内解析了实时或动态选择的语义资源，且缺乏明显的不可变绑定，存在潜在风险。
2. SemIso原型系统能够高效地在中间件层检查和强制执行语义隔离保证。
3. 该系统通过微秒级的性能开销，成功阻断不兼容的资源访问和分支合并，验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: AI工作流需要专门的语义隔离机制以应对动态环境带来的不一致性。提出的隔离级别体系和SemIso系统为检测和防止此类异常提供了有效解决方案，证明了在中间件层实施细粒度语义约束的可行性与高效性。

Abstract: An AI execution can now outlive the environment in which it began. What once fit inside one model call increasingly unfolds across pauses, retries, branches, subagents, and model-selected tools. Meanwhile, prompts, model aliases, indexes, policies, and tools are deployed independently: stable names can acquire new behavior, and workflows can discover resources only after they start. The workflow can therefore combine saved state with changed assumptions, producing an internally inconsistent result even when every call succeeds. This is an isolation problem: database transactions constrain concurrent data updates, but workflow checkpointing provides no corresponding contract for concurrent changes to an AI workflow's semantic environment.
  We define four automatically detectable anomalies: semantic read skew, compatibility skew, context escape, and merge skew. To control which anomalies are allowed, we derive a partial order of isolation levels, from Semantic Read Committed to Semantic Snapshot Isolation, by combining three independent guarantees: resource stability, cross-resource compatibility, and continuation inheritance. In a conservative source audit of the 100 most-starred public repositories with executable LangGraph code, we find that 7.4 percent of codebases with durable workflows resolve live or dynamically selected semantic resources within the same workflow, without an evident immutable binding. We show that these guarantees can be checked and enforced efficiently in middleware. Our prototype, SemIso, propagates semantic context and blocks incompatible resources and branch merges with microsecond-scale checks.

</details>


### [274] [Filtered Vector Search in a Disaggregated Lakehouse: Composing Table-Format Pruning with Per-File ANN](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05441)
*Rakesh Jain,Thomas Griffin,Syed Zawad*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 提出一种在Apache Iceberg湖仓表中原生嵌入IVF索引的方法，利用现有的文件裁剪机制（分区、区域映射）结合每文件的ANN搜索，实现高效的过滤向量查询，无需新增过滤算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决近似最近邻（ANN）搜索与结构化数据过滤通常分离的问题，避免将专用向量索引与单独过滤步骤拼凑在一起，旨在利用湖仓引擎成熟的文件裁剪能力来加速带过滤条件的向量检索。

Method: 1. 将IVF索引嵌入每个Parquet文件的页脚中；2. 查询时先通过谓词进行文件级裁剪，仅在剩余文件上运行IVF ANN搜索；3. 采用分布式、非破坏性的元数据替换方式构建索引；4. 使用 rendezvous-hashed 每文件缓存优化对象存储读取延迟。

Result: 在1150万x768的数据集上，预热IVF搜索比暴力搜索快约32倍（召回率>=0.90）；在IBM Granite真实嵌入数据集上，经过连接过滤并物化为区域分区布局后，速度比查询时连接快近两个数量级（约94倍，从14.7秒降至157毫秒）。

Conclusion: 该方法的核心收益来自文件裁剪，要求过滤列具有文件级局部性，且仅对纯分区列安全地将谓词推入搜索。研究还总结了将ANN集成到湖仓引擎中的失效模式及适用条件。

Abstract: Approximate nearest-neighbor (ANN) search increasingly runs alongside structured data - "find the 10 nearest documents where tenant='acme' AND lang='en'" - yet similarity and filtering are usually bolted together: a specialized vector index for one, a separate filter step for the other. We ask what happens when both live inside an open lakehouse table (Apache Iceberg over Parquet on object storage), where the engine already owns a mature file-pruning stack (partition pruning, zone-maps, a bitmap index). We embed an IVF index in place in each Parquet file's footer and make filtered vector queries fast not with a new filtering algorithm but by composing the table's existing file pruning with per-file ANN: the planner prunes data files by the predicate first, then runs IVF only over the survivors. The index is built distributed and non-destructively - a metadata-only Iceberg replace that every other engine still reads - and a rendezvous-hashed per-file cache keeps object-store read latency from swamping the algorithmic win. The payoff comes entirely from file pruning. On an 11.5M x 768 table, warm IVF search is ~32x faster than brute force at recall@10 >= 0.90, a selective predicate having pruned 355 of 444 data files before ANN runs; on 5M real IBM Granite embeddings, a filter arriving across a join prunes four of five region partitions and runs nearly two orders of magnitude (~94x: 14.7 s -> 157 ms) faster than the query-time join at identical top-k, once the reduction is materialized into a region-partitioned layout. We characterize when the composition pays off - it requires file-level locality on the filter column, and the residual predicate is only safe to push into the search over a provably pure (partitioned) column, not a merely sorted one - and report the failure modes we hit bolting ANN onto a lakehouse engine.

</details>


### [275] [Window Function Optimization: Co-Evaluation and Other Techniques](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06043)
*Daniel Lindner,Felix Naumann,Alberto Lerner*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出了一套针对SQL窗口函数优化的推理框架，包括Frame Analysis、Partition Analysis和Co-Evaluation策略，解决了现有优化技术在条件不满足时失效的问题。在开源引擎中测试显示，这些优化不会降低性能，且能使特定常见查询提速最高40.7倍，数据量越大增益越显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管窗口函数是SQL中最具表达力的特性之一，但关于其优化的研究相对较少。现有的优化技术（如谓词下推）仅在理想条件下有效，一旦条件稍有偏差便不再适用，这种局限性并非根本性的，而是由于缺乏统一的推理框架所致。

Method: 作者构建了一个窗口函数优化的推理框架，引入了三种核心技术：Frame Analysis（帧分析）、Partition Analysis（分区分析）以及一种新的执行策略Co-Evaluation（协同评估）。此外，还将结果整理为窗口函数的代数等价性表，并在开源数据库引擎中进行实证测试。

Result: 提出的优化技术在测试中从未损害性能。对于某些常见查询，性能提升了高达40.7倍。随着数据表规模的增大，性能提升效果更加明显。Co-Evaluation策略尤其允许在谓词依赖于窗口函数结果的情况下进行早期评估。

Conclusion: 通过建立完善的推理框架和创新执行策略，可以突破窗口函数优化的现有局限，显著提升复杂SQL查询的执行效率，且该方法具有通用性和安全性（无负向影响）。

Abstract: Window functions are among the most expressive features of modern SQL. Surprisingly, relatively little has been written about their optimization. Some techniques exist, such as pushing predicates through a window under ideal conditions, but known optimizations no longer apply when those conditions are even slightly unmet. We show that these limitations are not fundamental, but persist because a reasoning framework for window function optimization has been missing. We provide such a framework, introducing techniques we call Frame Analysis, Partition Analysis, and a new execution strategy called Co-Evaluation. These clarify when and how optimizations can be applied. Co-Evaluation, in particular, allows early evaluation of predicates even when they depend on the window function's result. We present each technique and organize the results as a table of algebraic equivalences for window functions. We test these optimizations in an open-source engine, where they never hurt performance and make certain common queries up to 40.7 times faster, with larger tables yielding larger gains.

</details>


### [276] [PLB: Priority-Aware Load Balancing for Replicated Databases under Constrained Resources](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06140)
*Belkis Djeffal,Pierre Bourhis,Romain Rouvoy*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 提出PLB，一种基于JDBC驱动程序的优先级感知负载均衡器，通过在固定资源下控制客户端会话到数据库副本的分配来实现优先级差异化服务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据库系统通常对所有会话一视同仁，而在容量固定且负载争用的场景下，面临过度配置资源或让低优先级用户承受巨大延迟增加的艰难权衡。

Method: 将副本按用户组（高级与免费）进行分区，并利用基于负载的资源借用机制：高优先级用户在有空闲容量时可借用资源，同时控制对低优先级用户的影响。

Result: 在OLAP工作负载下的评估显示：相比静态专用分区，PLB使集群CPU利用率从约35%提升至70%以上，同时保持接近最佳专用分配的延迟；相比完全共享的轮询池，PLB平均降低高优先级中位延迟12%（最高28%），同时将低优先级中位开销控制在11%左右，整体中位延迟接近轮询水平。

Conclusion: PLB通过动态的副本分配策略，在固定资源约束下有效平衡了资源利用率与不同优先级用户的性能需求，优于传统的静态分区和完全共享方案。

Abstract: Priority-differentiated services are a standard way for applications to offer different levels of performance, but database systems still often treat all sessions the same way. When database capacity is fixed, meaning replicas cannot be added on demand, and the workload becomes contended, this creates a difficult trade-off between over-provisioning resources and letting lower-priority users experience much larger slowdowns. In such settings, we propose enforcing priority by controlling how client sessions are assigned to database replicas.
  We present PLB, a priority-aware load balancer implemented as a JDBC driver that enforces priority differentiation through replica assignment under fixed resources. PLB partitions replicas by user group, premium versus freemium, and uses load-based borrowing so that higher-priority users can use idle capacity when available, while degradation for lower-priority users remains controlled. We evaluate PLB on a replicated read-only cluster under OLAP workloads. Compared with static dedicated per-priority partitions, PLB keeps utilization above about 70% in settings where fixed partitions can reduce cluster-wide CPU utilization to about 35%, while maintaining latencies close to those of the best dedicated allocation. Compared with a fully shared round-robin pool, PLB lowers high-priority median latency by about 12% on average, with improvements of up to 28%, while keeping the low-priority median overhead around 11% and overall median latency close to round-robin.

</details>


### [277] [Tytan: Interactive Neurosymbolic Construction of Analytic Semantic Schemas from Relational Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06331)
*Donna Hooshmand,Shubham Shahi,Cameron Barrie,Abhratanu Dutta,Marko Sterbentz,Harper Pack,Kristian J. Hammond*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 提出TYTAN系统，结合符号分析与LLM语义推理，自动从关系数据库构建分析语义模式，解决人工编写语义层的瓶颈问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前数据语义层通常需手动编写，存在知识获取瓶颈，限制了分析系统的可扩展性，使非技术用户依赖专家，且易出错。

Method: TYTAN系统结合数据库的符号分析与基于大语言模型（LLM）的语义推理，用于实体提议、角色分配和命名；在证据模糊时通过自然语言提问向用户寻求澄清。

Result: 在八个数据库上的评估显示：覆盖率达100%；所有生成的检索指令执行正确（1678/1678）；语义角色与参考标准一致率达92-100%；在盲测中完全恢复实体结构并满足独立标注者的期望。

Conclusion: TYTAN能有效自动构建高质量的分析语义模式，显著提升数据可用性，减少对人工专家的依赖，并验证了其在真实和基准领域中的功能效用。

Abstract: From natural-language query interfaces to automated report generation, data analysis tools need a description of the data: the real-world entities it contains, which columns function as measures or identifiers, and how tables connect into units of analysis. Today, this semantic layer is usually written by hand. This is a knowledge-acquisition bottleneck that limits the scalability of analytic systems, keeps non-technical users dependent on experts, and is itself error-prone. We present TYTAN, a system for automatically constructing an analytic semantic schema from a relational database and, when available, a short user-provided description. TYTAN combines symbolic analysis of the database with LLM-based semantic inference for entity proposal, role assignment, and naming. When the evidence leaves a decision ambiguous, TYTAN asks the user a targeted natural-language question. We evaluate TYTAN on eight databases spanning real-world and benchmark domains along the three axes that define a schema's functional utility: (i) coverage, are all important entities and features captured?; (ii) retrieval correctness, do the schema's instructions actually reach the data; and (iii) characterization accuracy, are semantic types correct? Across the seven reference domains, TYTAN reaches every entity, attribute, and aggregable feature of the expert-corrected reference schemas (100% coverage). Additionally, 100% of its retrieval instructions execute correctly (1,678 of 1,678 self-generated claims), and semantic roles agree with the reference on 92-100% of matched attributes. Checking the underlying data showed the small disagreement is in the reference, not in TYTAN. On a held-out blind test (a live, ten-table database with no declared keys), TYTAN recovers the full entity structure with verified keys and satisfies 100% of the satisfiable expectations of five independent blind annotators.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [278] [BioMedJImpact: A Comprehensive Dataset and LLM Pipeline for AI Engagement and Scientific Impact Analysis of Biomedical Journals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05227)
*Ruiyu Wang,Yuzhang Xie,Xiao Hu,Carl Yang,Jiaying Lu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文发布了BioMedJImpact数据集，通过分析174万篇生物医学文章，揭示了作者团队规模与AI参与度对期刊影响力的不同影响模式：团队规模越大引用越高，而AI参与度仅在2019年前与影响因子正相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有资源难以捕捉协作模式和人工智能（AI）研究如何共同塑造生物医学领域的期刊声望，缺乏将两者结合的大规模评估工具。

Method: 构建包含2,744种期刊、1.74百万篇文章的生物医学数据集；利用LLM三阶段流程提取“AI参与度”指标；分析协作强度与AI参与度在两个时间段（2016-2019, 2020-2023）对科学影响力的联合影响，并通过人工评估验证LLM管道的一致性。

Result: 1. 作者团队较大的期刊倾向于获得更高的引用影响力；2. AI参与度仅在与2019年及之前的子集数据中显示出与影响因子的正相关关系；3. LLM管道在AI相关性检测和子分类方面与人工评估具有高度一致性。

Conclusion: BioMedJImpact不仅提供了生物医学与AI交叉领域的全面数据集，还提供了一个经过验证的、可扩展的内容感知科学计量学分析框架。

Abstract: Assessing journal impact is central to scholarly communication, yet existing resources rarely capture how collaboration and artificial intelligence (AI) research jointly shape venue prestige in biomedicine. We present BioMedJImpact, a large-scale, biomedical-oriented dataset built from 1.74 million PubMed Central articles across 2,744 journals. BioMedJImpact integrates bibliometric indicators, collaboration features, and an LLM-derived AI engagement rate, defined as the proportion of AI-related articles within each journal-year. Specifically, AI engagement rate is extracted through a reproducible three-stage LLM pipeline. We analyze how collaboration intensity and AI engagement rate jointly influence scientific impact across two temporal subsets (2016-2019, 2020-2023). Two main patterns emerge: journals with larger author teams tend to have higher citation impact, while AI engagement rate is positively associated with Impact Factor only in the 2019 subset. To validate the LLM pipeline for deriving the AI engagement rate, we conduct human evaluation, confirming substantial agreement in AI relevance detection and consistent subfield classification. Together, BioMedJImpact provides both a comprehensive dataset at the interface of biomedicine and AI and a validated framework for scalable, content-aware scientometric analysis. Code and dataset are available at https://github.com/JonathanWry/BioMedJImpact.

</details>


### [279] [From Trajectories to Evidence: Auditable Experimental Records for Industrial Research Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05235)
*Zijie Zhuang,Changxin Lao,Pengbo Xu,Hanwen Xu,Ruochen Yang,Yingzhi He,Peng Zhang,Jiangxia Cao,Yusheng Huang,Guohong Mu,Jian Liang,Ruiming Tang,Shuang Yang,Zhaojie Liu,Wenwu Ou,Kun Gai*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种“轨迹到证据转换”框架，通过生成-验证-修复流程和混合LLM控制器，对工业推荐系统中的多轮机器学习实验轨迹进行有界验证和声明资格认定，以确保证据的有效性和可审计性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在工业推荐设置中，研究人员保留多轮机器学习实验的轨迹以指导后续决策，但已完成的轨迹并不自动构成有效证据，因为生成的工件可能缺乏支持或不完整，执行的轮次可能无效或存在混淆，且后期修改可能掩盖早期发现。因此，需要明确已完成的研究过程究竟确立了什么。

Method: 引入一个基于证据的框架，结合后果性工件的有界验证和执行后的声明资格认定。具体包括：1) 上下文隔离的“生成-验证-修复”流程，在发布前检查工件的证据违规和缺失的下游要求；2) 执行后的有效性和归属权检查，跨轮次整合证据，将干预级声明分类为可操作的修复、诊断守卫或保留的发现，并保留具有明确来源和适用边界的可审计记录；3) 使用混合LLM辅助控制器根据可用目标证据应用、推迟或拒绝记录。

Result: 记录审计揭示了哪些声明通过了资格认定，下游诊断表明“肯定适用性判断”是测试控制器的瓶颈。在论文到目标的适应过程中，后期轮次通常优于第一轮，但最终轮次经常不如早期的最佳表现，暴露出轨迹演变的非单调性。通过完整工作流产生的候选方案相对于部署基线产生了积极的在线提升。

Conclusion: 该框架有效地将研究轨迹转化为可信证据，尽管轨迹演变是非单调的，但经过严格验证和资格认定的工作流能产生比基线更优的结果，强调了证据 grounding 和可审计性在复杂机器学习实验中的重要性。

Abstract: Research agents increasingly conduct multi-round machine-learning experiments in industrial recommendation settings and retain the resulting trajectories to guide later decisions. Yet a completed trajectory is not automatically evidence: generated artifacts may be unsupported or incomplete, executed rounds may be invalid or confounded, and later modifications may obscure earlier findings. We study \textbf{trajectory-to-evidence conversion}, asking what a completed research process has actually established. We introduce an evidence-grounded framework that couples bounded verification of consequential artifacts with post-execution claim qualification. A context-isolated generate--verify--repair process checks artifacts for evidence violations and missing downstream requirements before release. After execution, validity and attribution checks consolidate evidence across rounds, qualify intervention-level claims as actionable repairs, diagnostic guards, or withheld findings, and preserve admitted claims as auditable records with explicit provenance and applicability boundaries. A hybrid LLM-assisted controller subsequently applies, defers, or rejects records based on available target evidence. Record audits characterize which claims survive qualification, while downstream diagnostics identify affirmative applicability judgment as a bottleneck for the tested controller. Across paper-to-target adaptations, later rounds often improve on the first, while final rounds frequently underperform an earlier best, exposing non-monotonic trajectory evolution. Candidates produced through the complete workflow also yielded positive online lifts relative to deployed baselines.

</details>


### [280] [Cross-platform epistemic verification for improving factual reliability in AI-generated news summarization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05302)
*Zhuo Xie,Haoze Ni*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出MECV框架，通过聚合多源异构证据（原文、维基百科、开放网络）及多LLM陪审团共识机制，对AI新闻摘要进行事后幻觉校正，提升事实一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决AI生成新闻摘要中的幻觉问题，克服单一检索通道的局限性，提高生成内容的 factual reliability（事实可靠性）。

Method: 1. 多源证据聚合：整合源文档、Wikipedia和开放网络检索；2. 多LLM陪审团机制：利用验证器模型间的矛盾感知共识评分评估事实可靠性；3. 迭代最小编辑修订：对潜在无支持声明进行修正。

Result: 在SummEdits基准测试上，使用GPT-4o-mini和DeepSeek-Chat作为验证器陪审团，Qwen-Plus作为协调器，实验证明MECV在保持语义结构的同时显著提高了事实一致性。

Conclusion: 跨异构证据源的一致性可作为识别AI生成摘要中事实不确定性的有效信号，该框架为可信AI和自动化新闻业提供了多源验证的解决方案。

Abstract: This study proposes Multi-source Evidence Consen- sus Verification (MECV), a post-hoc hallucination cor- rection framework for AI-generated news summariza- tion. Instead of depending on a single retrieval channel, MECV aggregates evidence from multiple heterogeneous sources, including the source document, Wikipedia, and open-web retrieval. The framework further incorporates a multi-LLM jury mechanism that estimates factual reliabil- ity through contradiction-aware consensus scoring across verifier models.
